&lt;project_context>
# 项目: gen-depth-contamination

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: autodl-4090d
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh autodl-4090d  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：修复 HuggingFace 下载失败，重启 mvp_pilot_sat_v1 实验

### 背景
mvp_pilot_sat_v1 在 Step 2（C4 数据下载）卡住，HuggingFace 代理连接失败。已 kill PID 4173。需要修复环境变量后重启。

### 服务器信息
- SSH Host: `autodl-4090d`
- 环境: aml-train 镜像, conda base
- 激活方式: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
- 项目路径: `/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination`
- 脚本: `/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/scripts/run_pipeline.py`
- 模型路径: `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct/Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct`

### Step 1: 诊断（ssh_read_only）
```bash
# 检查残留进程
ps aux | grep run_pipeline | grep -v grep

# 检查已有数据（如果 depth_0.jsonl 已部分下载）
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/
wc -l /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/depth_0.jsonl 2>/dev/null || echo "NO_DATA"

# 检查上次日志的错误
tail -30 ~/runs/gen-depth-contamination/mvp_pilot_sat_v1.log 2>/dev/null
```

### Step 2: 创建启动包装脚本（ssh_execute）
在服务器上创建 `/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/run_mvp.sh`，**所有环境变量写入脚本**，不依赖 shell session：

```bash
#!/bin/bash
set -e

# AutoDL 网络加速
source /etc/network_turbo

# SSL 证书（AutoDL 代理需要）
export REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt
export SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt

# HuggingFace 配置
export HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache
export HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1
export HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com

# Conda 环境
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh
conda activate base

cd /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination
python scripts/run_pipeline.py --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct/Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct
```

注意：**同时设置 HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com 作为备用**，防止 HF 官方仍不通。

### Step 3: 验证环境变量 + HF 连通性（ssh_execute）
在提交实验前先做快速验证：
```bash
source /etc/network_turbo && \
export REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt && \
export SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt && \
export HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1 && \
export HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com && \
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && \
python -c "
from datasets import load_dataset
ds = load_dataset('allenai/c4', 'en', split='validation', streaming=True)
sample = next(iter(ds))
print(f'C4 OK: {sample[\"text\"][:80]}...')
"
```

如果这步失败：
- 检查 `curl -I https://hf-mirror.com` 是否通
- 试 `export HF_ENDPOINT=https://huggingface.co` 回退官方源
- 如果都不通，检查 `/etc/network_turbo` 是否正确配置了代理

### Step 4: 清理旧数据（如果有损坏的部分下载）
如果 depth_0.jsonl 存在但行数不足 5000，删除它让 pipeline 重新下载：
```bash
lines=$(wc -l < /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/depth_0.jsonl 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
if [ "$lines" -lt 5000 ]; then
    rm -f /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/depth_0.jsonl
    echo "Removed incomplete depth_0.jsonl ($lines lines)"
fi
```

### Step 5: 提交实验
用 `submit_training_job` 重新提交：
- exp_id: `mvp_pilot_sat_v1`
- project: `gen-depth-contamination`
- server: `autodl-4090d`
- gpu_id: `cuda:0`
- command: `bash /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/run_mvp.sh`

### 产出
1. 报告修复了什么问题
2. 确认 C4 数据能正常流式下载
3. 确认实验已重新提交并在运行

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The new run is hitting the same error — it's still going to `huggingface.co` instead of `hf-mirror.com`. The `HF_ENDPOINT` env var in the wrapper script isn't being picked up by the `datasets` library, or the `datasets` library is using the hardcoded URL.

Let me think about this:
1. The `HF_ENDPOINT` env var is supposed to redirect HuggingFace Hub API calls to the mirror
2. But looking at the error, it's still hitting `huggingface.co` — meaning `HF_ENDPOINT` isn't being respected


3. The timestamps show the new run started at 19:49:30 and failed immediately at 19:49:33 with the same proxy error

So the issue is that `datasets` uses `huggingface_hub` under the hood, which should respect `HF_ENDPOINT`, but it's not working. Either the `datasets` library version doesn't support it, or the `network_turbo` proxy is overriding the endpoint configuration.

Let me kill this job and debug by checking what environment variables are actually set in the running process, particularly whether `HF_ENDPOINT` is being picked up or if `network_turbo` is setting conflicting proxy variables.
```

```text
3. `hf-mirror.com` is NOT in the `no_proxy` list

- `HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com` should redirect API calls to hf-mirror.com


- But the proxy is intercepting these requests and can't handle them, OR the `HF_ENDPOINT` setting isn't being respected at all

Looking at the error more carefully, it's actually hitting `huggingface.co` instead of `hf-mirror.com`, which suggests `HF_ENDPOINT` isn't being applied. I need to verify whether it was actually set in the environment and check how the `datasets` library version handles endpoint resolution—with `huggingface_hub` 0.36.2 it should work, but something's preventing it.

The real issue might be in how the `run_mvp.sh` script was constructed: I sourced `network_turbo` first, then exported `HF_ENDPOINT`, but I need to verify the script was written correctly and that the environment variable is actually being passed through. Alternatively, the proxy configuration itself might be the problem—either the proxy can't reach `huggingface.co` but should be routing through `hf-mirror.com`, or I need to adjust the `no_proxy` settings to handle this correctly.
```

_source line 155 · 2026-05-12T12:01:00.777Z_

SSH 连接持续失败（"No existing session"）。根据 AutoDL 的已知行为，实例可能重启了导致 SSH 端口变更。

请去 AutoDL 控制台确认实例状态，如果端口已变更，更新 `~/.ssh/config` 中 `autodl-4090d` 的端口配置后，我会继续执行。

当前进展：
1. 已确认原始问题：`HF_ENDPOINT` 虽然被正确设置，但 C4 数据集文件列表巨大（数千个文件），通过 AutoDL 代理的 tree listing API 调用极慢/超时
2. 已创建 `run_mvp.sh` 包装脚本，环境变量配置正确
3. 已清理 C4 缓存

**待恢复连接后的修复方案**：修改 `run_pipeline.py` 的 `prepare_seed_data` 函数，避免让 `datasets` 库解析 C4 全量文件树。改为直接指定单个 validation shard 文件路径（如 `data_files={'train': 'en/c4-validation.00000-of-00008.json.gz'}`），或改用 ModelScope 作为数据源。
